      "recordSeq": 108,

      "text": {
        "edge-note": {
          "text": "Counterfoil R-17Your edge: HintedDisclosure: Category shared; exact detail private.Interest: Marcus cares.Exact evidence and observationsToday's signed counterfoil says tomorrow's Contra collection moved to loading gate C, between 07:00 and 07:30, using docket R-17.Marcus cares.Marcus has told you he cares about R-17.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "r17-rate": {
          "text": "Marcus's extra charge: 16% of new principal.",
          "visible": true,

          "textParts": 1
        },

          "text": "",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,

          "text": "$7 · 8% of new credit",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1

          "text": "Standard charge without R-17: $13 · 16%",

          "textPart": 1,

        "draft-summary": {
          "text": "Keep $40 cash. Repay $87 in 7 days ($80 credit + $7 extra). Old debt stays owed.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "information-reason": {
          "text": "Attach counterfoil R-17; it stays yours until you confirm the exchange. Hint first to learn whether Marcus cares.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "player-preview": {
          "text": "All right… I propose 2 Contra units, $40 upfront, $80 in new credit at your extra charge, due in 7 days. This is in addition to my existing debt. I offer counterfoil R-17 and its exact detail with these terms, to be transferred only if we both agree. You are free to decline.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1

          "text": "Ready · review, then send",
          "visible": true,

          "text": "You: Okay… I have a document about a collection change. Do you care about that information? I'm keeping the detail covered.",

          "text": "Yes. I care about that information. Keep R-17's exact detail covered if you want to price it into the deal.",

      "text": {
        "edge-note": {
          "text": "Counterfoil R-17Your edge: HintedDisclosure: Category shared; exact detail private.Interest: Marcus cares.Exact evidence and observationsToday's signed counterfoil says tomorrow's Contra collection moved to loading gate C, between 07:00 and 07:30, using docket R-17.Marcus cares.Marcus has told you he cares about R-17.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "r17-rate": {
          "text": "Marcus's extra charge: 16% of new principal.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "current-offer": {
          "text": "",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "extra-display": {
          "text": "$4 · 8% of new credit",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "extra-comparison": {
          "text": "Standard charge without R-17: $8 · 16%",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "draft-summary": {
          "text": "Keep $10 cash. Repay $54 in 7 days ($50 credit + $4 extra). Old debt stays owed.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "information-reason": {
          "text": "Attach counterfoil R-17; it stays yours until you confirm the exchange. Hint first to learn whether Marcus cares.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "player-preview": {
          "text": "All right… I propose 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit at your extra charge, due in 7 days. This is in addition to my existing debt. I offer counterfoil R-17 and its exact detail with these terms, to be transferred only if we both agree. You are free to decline.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "preview-state": {
          "text": "Ready · review, then send",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "player-line": {
          "text": "You: Okay… I have a document about a collection change. Do you care about that information? I'm keeping the detail covered.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "npc-line": {
          "text": "Yes. I care about that information. Keep R-17's exact detail covered if you want to price it into the deal.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "face-caption": {
          "text": "Both brows lift above forward-looking eyes; the mouth holds a restrained smile.",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "encounter-status": {
          "text": "Conversation open",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "notice": {
          "text": "Response received. Review what changed, then choose your next move.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "latest-feedback": {
          "text": "Yes. I care about that information. The detail remains private; R-17 remains held. The extra charge is 16%.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "agreement": {
          "text": "Cash: $80. Your Contra: 0. Total owed: $250.",
          "visible": false,

    {
      "authority": "ui-observation",
      "recordSeq": 136,
      "reason": "preview",
      "turn": 1,
      "part": 1,
      "parts": 4,
      "text": {
        "edge-note": {
          "text": "Counterfoil R-17Your edge: HintedDisclosure: Category shared; exact detail private.Interest: Marcus cares.Exact evidence and observationsToday's signed counterfoil says tomorrow's Contra collection moved to loading gate C, between 07:00 and 07:30, using docket R-17.Marcus cares.Marcus has told you he cares about R-17.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "r17-rate": {
          "text": "Marcus's extra charge: 16% of new principal.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "current-offer": {
          "text": "",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "extra-display": {
          "text": "$4 · 8% of new credit",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "extra-comparison": {
          "text": "Standard charge without R-17: $8 · 16%",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "draft-summary": {
          "text": "Keep $10 cash. Repay $54 in 7 days ($50 credit + $4 extra). Old debt stays owed.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "information-reason": {
          "text": "Attach counterfoil R-17; it stays yours until you confirm the exchange. Hint first to learn whether Marcus cares.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "player-preview": {
          "text": "All right… I propose 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit at your extra charge, due in 7 days. This is in addition to my existing debt. I offer counterfoil R-17 and its exact detail with these terms, to be transferred only if we both agree. You are free to decline.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "preview-state": {
          "text": "Ready · review, then send",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "player-line": {
          "text": "You: Okay… I have a document about a collection change. Do you care about that information? I'm keeping the detail covered.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "npc-line": {
          "text": "Yes. I care about that information. Keep R-17's exact detail covered if you want to price it into the deal.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "face-caption": {
          "text": "Both brows lift above forward-looking eyes; the mouth holds a restrained smile.",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "encounter-status": {
          "text": "Conversation open",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "notice": {
          "text": "Response received. Review what changed, then choose your next move.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "latest-feedback": {
          "text": "Yes. I care about that information. The detail remains private; R-17 remains held. The extra charge is 16%.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "agreement": {
          "text": "Cash: $80. Your Contra: 0. Total owed: $250.",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "outcome": {
          "text": "",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "revisit": {
          "text": "Revisit earlier pointsHintAlready addressed; repeating cannot renew this step's progress.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "face-review-pair": {
          "text": "",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "conversation": {
          "text": "Turn 1You: Okay… I have a document about a collection change. Do you care about that information? I'm keeping the detail covered.Marcus ‘Broker’ Hill: Yes. I care about that information. Keep R-17's exact detail covered if you want to price it into the deal.Yes. I care about that information. The detail remains private; R-17 remains held. The extra charge is 16%.Inspect both reactions",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        }
      },

### Turn 2 · ACCEPT

You: All right… I propose 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit at your extra charge, due in 7 days. This is in addition to my existing debt. I offer counterfoil R-17 and its exact detail with these terms, to be transferred only if we both agree. You are free to decline.

Marcus: R-17 has value to me. I'll go to 8% extra for this information offer, with the exchange completed on confirmation. Review these terms: 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit plus $4 extra due in 7 days. The terms include the information exchange. I receive the private detail only when you confirm. I can work with a clear approach like that.

### Turn 3 · AGREED

You: Okay… I accept the current offer: 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit plus $4 extra due in 7 days. My existing debt remains separate. I deliver the agreed information: Today's signed counterfoil says tomorrow's Contra collection moved to loading gate C, between 07:00 and 07:30, using docket R-17.

Marcus: Agreed. The stock is yours on the terms you just confirmed. The old account stays on the books.

          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "player-line": {
          "text": "You: Okay… I accept the current offer: 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit plus $4 extra due in 7 days. My existing debt remains separate. I deliver the agreed information: Today's signed counterfoil says tomorrow's Contra collection moved to loading gate C, between 07:00 and 07:30, using docket R-17.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "npc-line": {
          "text": "Agreed. The stock is yours on the terms you just confirmed. The old account stays on the books.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "face-caption": {
          "text": "The brows stay low above forward-looking eyes; the mouth opens into a broad toothy smile.",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "encounter-status": {
          "text": "Agreement reached",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,

        },

          "text": "Conversation complete. Review the result or replay the reactions.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "latest-feedback": {
          "text": "Counterfoil R-17 and its agreed detail have now been delivered to Marcus with the completed transaction.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "agreement": {
          "text": "Contra acquired2Cash remaining$10New repayment$54 in 7 daysTotal owed$304Old debt: $250. New credit: $50 + $4 extra. The old debt is still owed.R-17 saved you $4 on the extra charge.Accepted termsContra units2Cash now70New principal50Additional repayment4Repay within (days)7Counterfoil R-17 and its exact detail; transferred only on confirmation.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "outcome": {
          "text": "Marcus's confidence in your commitment rose relative to the opening.Tension in the conversation fell relative to the opening.",
          "visible": true,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "revisit": {
          "text": "Revisit earlier points",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "face-review-pair": {
          "text": "",
          "visible": false,
          "textPart": 1,
          "textParts": 1
        },
        "conversation": {
          "text": "Turn 1You: Okay… I have a document about a collection change. Do you care about that information? I'm keeping the detail covered.Marcus ‘Broker’ Hill: Yes. I care about that information. Keep R-17's exact detail covered if you want to price it into the deal.Yes. I care about that information. The detail remains private; R-17 remains held. The extra charge is 16%.Inspect both reactionsTurn 2You: All right… I propose 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit at your extra charge, due in 7 days. This is in addition to my existing debt. I offer counterfoil R-17 and its exact detail with these terms, to be transferred only if we both agree. You are free to decline.Marcus ‘Broker’ Hill: R-17 has value to me. I'll go to 8% extra for this information offer, with the exchange completed on confirmation. Review these terms: 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit plus $4 extra due in 7 days. The terms include the information exchange. I receive the private detail only when you confirm. I can work with a clear approach like that.Marcus values R-17. The extra charge on the current information offer is 8%. The document and its detail remain yours until confirmation; removing it from a later offer removes this value.Inspect both reactionsTurn 3You: Okay… I accept the current offer: 2 Contra units, $70 upfront, $50 in new credit plus $4 extra due in 7 days. My existing debt remains separate. The stock is yours on the terms you just confirmed. The old account stays on the books.Counterfoil R-17 and its agreed detail have now been delivered to Marcus with the completed transaction.Inspect both reactions",
          "visible": false,
